 1013 |  2 adb_routines.s     34 | Comment     | 00 |         |    0 | 00/0613               | *  The IIgs communicates with the ADB chip through the Keygloo.
 1014 |  2 adb_routines.s     35 | Comment     | 00 |         |    0 | 00/0613               | *  The Keygloo allows communication between the uC and the system processor.
